## `AFG-3315-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `ENG`
- **Role:** MT-PT-Marine Inspector
- **Scenario:** Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Propeller Shaft Sag-and-Gap Alignment & Crankshaft Deflection
- **Intro:** MT-PT-Marine Inspector — Steady-State Process Parameter Micro-Tuning & Drift Compensation Calibration SOP for Propeller Shaft Sag-and-Gap Alignment & Crankshaft Deflection — Pre-Operation Safety and Lockout/Tagout (LOTO) Verification: Shaft rotation must be 0° (no movement) during all measurements. Ambient temperature in engine room must be 20°C ± 2°C…
- **Key tolerance:** Shaft rotation must be 0° (no movement) during all measurements. Ambient temperature in engine room must be 20°C ± 2°C to avoid thermal expansion effects.
- **Standards:** `ISO 1940-1:2003`, `ISO 10816-3:2009`, `IMO Resolution A.749(18) Code on Intact Stability for All Types `, `Afghanistan National Standards Authority (ANSA) Technical Regula`
- **Detail pages:** [EN](https://sop.go2abroad.space/en/sop/AFG-3315-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [中文](https://sop.go2abroad.space/zh/sop/AFG-3315-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [DE](https://sop.go2abroad.space/de/sop/AFG-3315-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [日本語](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ja/sop/AFG-3315-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [FR](https://sop.go2abroad.space/fr/sop/AFG-3315-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [ES](https://sop.go2abroad.space/es/sop/AFG-3315-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [PT](https://sop.go2abroad.space/pt/sop/AFG-3315-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [IT](https://sop.go2abroad.space/it/sop/AFG-3315-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [한국어](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ko/sop/AFG-3315-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [العربية](https://sop.go2abroad.space/ar/sop/AFG-3315-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html) · [हिन्दी](https://sop.go2abroad.space/hi/sop/AFG-3315-ENG-R3-ENG-SC-01.html)
